# PAPER_TRACEABILITY

原文 / 协议 → 原子需求 → 实现 → 测试 → 审查 的逐条覆盖账本。
本文件只做追溯索引，**不实现代码**，**不改论文**。

- 生成日期：2026-09-21

## 1. Field and category conventions

Required fields per row: `source_file`, `source_hash`, `section`, `line_start-line_end`, `requirement_summary`, `category`, `req_id`, `impl_file/symbol`, `entrypoint`, `verification_method`, `actual_result`, `review_ref`, `impl_status`, `local_verify_status`, `server_verify_status`.

Categories (exact):

| category | meaning |
|---|---|
| `executable_function` | must be implemented as callable code / CLI / adapter |
| `math_stat_definition` | formula, metric, or statistical object to implement faithfully |
| `experiment_protocol` | how to run, split, report, restrict, or decide; includes scientific-error limits |
| `hypothesis_to_test` | expected interval / falsifiable claim; **not** a required positive result |
| `background_citation` | related work, titles, historical narrative; no functional impl |
| `user_excluded` | RL, production gates/fallback, publication, host-exec of model code |

Status defaults: `impl_status=unimplemented` (or `n/a` / `excluded`); `actual_result=pending`; `review_ref=—`; local/server `pending` unless n/a.
Planned entrypoints follow `.planning/research/ARCHITECTURE.md`: `cli:{prepare,collect,label,fit,calibrate,intervene,repair,analyze}`.

## 4. Paper items missing from the original 16 requirements

Original 16 (`DATA-01`…`DECIDE-01`) are an index, not a complete paper cover. `.planning/REQUIREMENTS.md` later added 10 paper-derived atomics (`SURF-01`…`FAIL-01`). The following **paper-substantive items** are still outside those 16, or only loosely implied:

### 4.1 Already listed as paper-derived atomics (not in the original 16)

| req_id | paper locus | why the original 16 missed it |
|---|---|---|
| `SURF-01` | §2.3 L87 | `R^{surf}` 与值依赖分列，供符号替换；原 16 未单列表面提及 |
| `CONE-01` | §2.5 L121–125 | 脏锥公式与 Oracle/预测掩码分列；`REPAIR-01` 只写嫁接未写 cone |
| `ATTN-01` | 结果§8 L333 | 注意力均值 / Rollout / 头聚合及误差分组；`BASE-01` 仅点名未规定细节 |
| `VERB-01` | 结果§8 L335 | 自述四档与监督预算；`BASE-01` 提及但未钉四档协议 |
| `COST-01` | §3 L200, CHK15 | 首次索引 / Prefill / Decode / 探针 / 调度 / E2E 分列；`MODEL-01` 只写耗时 |
| `GEOM-01` | 附录 S3 L371–373 | 共同维 + Procrustes；`XFER-01` 管探针迁移不管几何附录 |
| `RETR-01` | 附录 S4 L375–377 | 检索余弦 vs 答案改变，不得删除 |
| `FIT-01` | 附录 S2 L363–369 | 两参数锥拟合 + 禁“定律”措辞 |
| `CONT-01` | 结果§9 L355 | 连续 k=1..5、序列校准、重新 Prefill |
| `FAIL-01` | 结果§9 L353 | 五类失效 + 验证器不回退；`REPAIR-01` 只写失败类型未写相图轴 |

| req_id | paper locus | requirement_summary |
|---|---|---|

| `HARDWARE-01` | §8.1 L417 | 8×5090 / 4×A100 分工，本机不冒充 GPU 验收 |

### 4.3 Original 16 that **do** absorb large paper blocks

`DATA-01/02/03`, `MEAS-01/02`, `MODEL-01`, `PROBE-01`, `BASE-01`, `XFER-01`, `CAUSAL-01/02`, `C3-01`, `REPAIR-01`, `OPS-01`, `QA-01`, `DECIDE-01` 覆盖了 T1–T4 适配、双标签、三时机采集、双头探针、公平基线、前瞻干预、P1–P3、无门控修复、CLI 与决策分流的主干。缺口主要在：**表面提及、脏锥/命题、三位置分叉、INLP 具名、边界超参、T2-noop 分层、Table1 基线切分、附录几何/拟合/检索、科学限制 REST-01/02/03、写作约束与清单逐条。**

## 5. Reverse index — production modules to map later

仓库当前无 `.py` 生产代码。下列模块来自 `ARCHITECTURE.md` / `STACK.md` / `OPS-01`，实现后必须回填本表的 `impl_file/symbol` 与行号，并做反向核验（每个生产符号 → 至少一条已授权需求）。

| planned_module | planned symbols / CLI | primary req_ids | later mapping notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `artifacts` | manifest, run_spec, shard resume, hash IDs | `OPS-01`, `DATA-03` | 不决定科学标签 |
| `tasks.igsm` | official export, mod-23, premise-span map | `DATA-01` | 与自建 DAG 夹具分来源 |
| `tasks.gsm_symbolic` | JSONL reader, formula sidecar, family_id | `DATA-02`, `DATA-03` | 无官方 parser |
| `tasks.gsm_plus` | test-only split, seed family | `DATA-02`, `DATA-03` | 禁止训练用途 |
| `tasks.hotpot` | supporting_facts ≠ full DAG | `DATA-02` | 单文档编辑须限制前提 |
| `tasks.musique` | composition_reference graph | `DATA-02` | 保留 answerable 配对 |
| `tasks.humaneval` | Spec-edit packs | `DATA-02`, `EXEC-01` | 项目派生名，非官方 Perturb |
| `tasks.noop` | T2-noop / iGSM noop pairs | `T2NOOP-01`, `C3-01` | 分层位置与相关度 |
| `tasks.decouple` | a=b / a≠b source pairs | `CAUSAL-02` | 干预前冻结响应矩阵 |
| `tasks.t4` | strategy/unsat/insufficient | `FAIL-01`, `DATA-02` | 类别不得由文件名推断 |
| `models.qwen3` | chat template, think IDs, resid_post | `MODEL-01`, `STACK-01` | d=4096, 36L |
| `models.r1_qwen2` | template already has `<think>` | `MODEL-01`, `STACK-01` | d=3584, 28L |
| `models.features` | pre_step / pre_value / post_step | `POS-01`, `MODEL-01` | 跨界 token 排除 |
| `models.rng` | explicit Generator decode loop | `MODEL-01`, `MEAS-02` | 不用 generate(generator=) |
| `events` | identity align, structural taxonomy | `MEAS-01`, `STRUCT-01` | 值不进身份 |
| `measure.labels` | R_task / R_behavior / R_surf / S / M | `MEAS-02`, `SURF-01`, `C3-01` | unknown 不作 0 |
| `measure.noise` | paired sham opportunities | `MEAS-02` | 无协议则 excess=null |
| `measure.to_csp` | TO, CSP, coverage | `MEAS-02`, `FIG1-01` | 干净事件来自任务图 |
| `probes.bilinear` | U,V r=64, dual heads, λ_FN=10 | `PROBE-01` | 未知掩码 |
| `probes.boundary_mlp` | 2-layer Hidden=256 ReLU | `BOUND-01` | 句法监督 |
| `probes.calibrate` | max score, order statistic, +inf | `PROP2-01` | 轨迹/序列单位 |
| `baselines.text` | prefix-matched text predictor | `BASE-01` | 公平前缀 |
| `baselines.attention` | mean / rollout / head agg | `ATTN-01` | 阈值在 dev |
| `baselines.verbalizer` | 0/5-shot / reflect / SFT | `VERB-01` | 监督档同样本 |
| `transfer` | direct / unlabeled / supervised | `XFER-01`, `GEOM-01` | 4096→3584 direct = N/A |
| `interventions.swap` | Π_Z residual patch, prospective | `CAUSAL-01`, `IE-01` | 首 token 前 |
| `interventions.inlp` | knock-out subspace | `INLP-01`, `CAUSAL-01` | 四项结局 |
| `interventions.rescue` | match / wrong-source / random | `CAUSAL-01` | 非恒等重建 |
| `interventions.controls` | C-rand, C-layer, matched norm | `CAUSAL-01` | 同批次 cohort |
| `c3.p1_p2_p3` | held-out AUC, paired Δ, Δacc | `C3-01` | 受 REST-02 |
| `repair.graft` | ungated slot executor | `REPAIR-01`, `TOPO-01` | 共用执行器 |

反向核验规则（实现后执行）：每个上述模块的公共函数、配置分支、CLI 子命令、训练/评估脚本、数据转换、指标和错误路径，都必须能关联到本表至少一条 `req_id`。未覆盖部分不得默认通过。

## 6. Scientific restriction index (must stay protocol)

| rest_id | forbidden inference | required reporter behavior | sources |
|---|---|---|---|
| `REST-01` | 步前可解码 ⇒ 依赖已经决定 / 不可逆选择 | 只报告三位置可解码性与 R7 分叉；不输出 already-decided 作为已证 | paper L110/L295; PROTO §1 C1; GOAL 五/14; PIT P4; FEAT L206 |
| `REST-02` | P3 失败 ⇒ 虚假依赖只是后果而非原因 | 分别报告 P2/P3；写“该干预未提供支持”，禁止自动因果句 | paper L168/L329; PROTO §5#4; GOAL 五/12; PIT P11/P12 |
| `REST-03` | 单前提/单点扫描 soundness ⇒ 任意联合编辑稳健 | 联合编辑反例夹具必须挡住对外 soundness 宣称 | paper Prop.1; GOAL 五/14; PIT P2 check; CONE-01 |

## 7. Maintenance

实现或审查每关闭一条需求时：回填 `impl_file/symbol`（含准确行号）、`entrypoint`、`verification_method` 命令、`actual_result`、`review_ref`、三项 status。
论文文件保持只读。若论文变更，须先重算 SHA-256 并更新 registry。
